  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子物质在远离平衡态时如何弛豫是多体物理中的核心开放问题，而现有的经典张量网络方法在后期已难以准确模拟。

Method: 使用包含256个量子比特的二维里德堡原子阵列，模拟二维横场伊辛模型在其相图范围内的弛豫过程。

Result: 除快速热化外，发现了两个新区域：一个是受有效XY模型支配的预热区域，另一个是表现出弛豫显著减慢的交叉区域；且量子模拟在不同系统尺寸下保持一致性，超越了经典方法的模拟能力。

Conclusion: 里德堡原子阵列可作为研究非平衡量子多体动力学中新物理现象的强大平台，具备超越经典模拟能力的潜力。

Abstract: How quantum matter relaxes far from equilibrium is a central open problem in many-body physics, and one for which analog quantum simulators are well positioned to move from confirming theory to discovering new physics. Here, we use a two-dimensional Rydberg atom array of 256 qubits to map the relaxation landscape of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model across its phase diagram. Beyond the expected rapid thermalization, we identify two further regimes. The first is a prethermal regime whose dynamics are governed by an effective XY model. The second, and most unexpected, is a crossover regime characterized by a slowdown in relaxation. This slowdown occurs precisely where state-of-the-art classical tensor-network methods lose control at late times, whereas the quantum simulation remains consistent across system sizes. These results establish Rydberg atom arrays as a platform for scientific discovery in nonequilibrium quantum many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Ultrafast quantum gate operations in a Kramers-Henneberger atom Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07185)
*A. Tasnim Aynul,C. Li,L. Cruz Rodriguez,C. Figueira de Morisson Faria*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并展示了Kramers-Henneberger（KH）原子作为一种用于超快单量子比特门操作的新型量子比特平台，实现了飞秒级量子门操作，比激光驱动的超导量子比特快六个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能够在极短时间内实现相干单量子比特门操作的新型量子计算平台，以突破现有技术的速度限制。

Method: 利用强激光场在KH参考系中构建由双势阱势的两个最低本征态定义的量子比特基，并通过弱共振控制场驱动实现相干门操作；使用完整的含时KH势进行全含时薛定谔方程模拟验证门操作性能。

Result: 成功演示了飞秒量级的Z门和S门操作，退相干时间长于门操作所需时间，完整六门单量子比特集合在时间平均两能级极限下得以实现，且模拟结果与时间平均模型高度一致，误差主要来源于结构化泄漏而非随机退相干。

Conclusion: KH原子为超快量子门操作提供了可行方案，首次在强场环境下实现了相干单量子比特门，具备向阿秒尺度发展的潜力，且可通过脉冲工程抑制主要误差通道。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate the Kramers-Henneberger KH) atom as a novel qubit platform for ultrafast single-qubit gate operations. In the KH frame, the time-averaged strong laser field engineers a double-well potential whose two lowest eigenstates define the qubit basis, so that the computational structure is created and maintained by the driving field itself. A weak resonant control field drives coherent gate operations: full time-dependent Schrödinger equation simulations with the complete time-dependent KH potential confirm a Z gate and S gate of the order of femtoseconds, six orders of magnitude faster than laser-driven superconducting qubit gates. Decoherence characterisation gives longer decoherence times than those required for the gate operations. The complete six-gate single-qubit set is demonstrated in the time-averaged two-level limit, with strong agreement between the full and time-averaged descriptions confirming that fidelity is limited by structured leakage rather than stochastic decoherence. In principle, this is an error channel suppressible through pulse engineering. These results constitute the first demonstration of coherent single-qubit gates in a strong-field setting, with a clear pathway toward attosecond-scale operations.

</details>


### [103] [Atomic correlation effects in collapse-induced spontaneous radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07205)
*Simone Manti,Nicola Bortolotti,Lajos Diósi,Kristian Piscicchia,Catalina Curceanu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于任意噪声下自发辐射率的通用表达式，系统地纳入了原子结构对坍缩模型预测的影响，适用于如Diósi-Penrose和连续自发局部化模型，并揭示了电荷关联引起的低能区新效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地约束量子坍缩模型的参数，需考虑在1-100 keV能量范围内原子结构对自发辐射的影响，因为该范围内的光子波长与原子尺度相当，导致发射行为具有模型依赖性。

Method: 推导了任意噪声条件下自发辐射率的一般表达式，通过发射体的径向分布结合具体坍缩模型来系统引入原子结构效应，并分析了电荷关联导致的抵消机制等新现象。

Result: 成功复现了已有模型在特定极限下的结果，发现了低能区由电荷关联引起的新抵消效应，并计算了锗和氙在Diósi-Penrose与CSL模型下的辐射率变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为考虑原子结构效应提供了统一方法，有助于建立更精确、材料相关的实验约束，提升对量子坍缩模型参数的限制能力。

Abstract: Collapse models introduce stochastic and nonlinear modifications in the quantum dynamics, predicting observable effects, such as spontaneous radiation from charged particles, which can be used to constrain their parameters. Recently, attention has focused on the 1-100 keV energy range, where the wavelength of the emitted photons becomes comparable to atomic dimensions, making the emission sensitive to atomic structure and leading to model-dependent behaviors that enable their discrimination. Here, we derive a general expression for the spontaneous emission rate for arbitrary noise, providing a framework that systematically incorporates the atomic structure through the radial distribution of the emitters, modulated by the specific collapse model. The formalism recovers previous results in the appropriate limits and naturally includes new low-energy effects, such as cancellation mechanisms arising from charge correlations. We evaluate the rates for germanium and xenon within the Diósi-Penrose and Continuous Spontaneous Localization models, showing how these correlations modify the predicted emission rates. This approach provides a unified framework to account for atomic effects and enables more robust, material-dependent experimental constraints on collapse model parameters.

</details>


### [104] [Existence of Kraus decomposition in infinite dimension via strongly-convergent direct process tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07207)
*Paul E. Lammert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于可分希尔伯特空间上完全正算子的Kraus分解的算法，并给出了强算子拓扑下收敛性的初等证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进标准非构造性证明，将抽象问题与实际的过程层析成像相结合。

Method: 逐个生成Kraus算子，每个比前一个至少多一个零矩阵元，逐步构造分解。

Result: 算法输出流提供了目标CP映射在递增子空间上的限制的一系列相容的Kraus分解，且级数在强算子拓扑下收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法为完全正算子的Kraus分解提供了构造性、可实现的路径，并增强了理论与实验的联系。

Abstract: An algorithm is presented for Kraus decomposition of a completely positive operator over separable (countably-infinite-dimensional) Hilbert spaces, together with an elementary proof that the generated sum convergences in strong-operator topology. This improves on the standard, nonconstructive, proof by fusing the abstract problem with practical process tomography. Kraus operators are generated one-by-one, each having one more guaranteed zero matrix entry than the previous one. In this way, the stream of outputs of the algorithm provides a coherent family of Kraus decompositions of restrictions of the target CP map to ever-larger subspaces.

</details>


### [105] [Origin of Long-Lived Nuclear Spin States and Coherences in Aliphatic Chains Revealed by Relaxation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07219)
*Danil A. Markelov,Kirill F. Sheberstov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Redfield弛豫理论揭示了脂肪链中离域长寿命态（LLSs）和集体零量子长寿命相干态（LLCs）的普遍结构，建立了适用于任意长度亚甲基链的构造框架，并阐明了手性与非手性分子中可访问的长寿命算符数量差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已在实验中观察到亚甲基网络中的LLSs和LLCs，但其在任意长度链中的起源和一般结构仍不清楚，本文旨在从理论上系统阐明其形成机制和普适规律。

Method: 基于主导的分子内双偶极-偶极弛豫机制，构建弛豫超算符的零本征值子空间（即长寿命子空间），并结合分子手性对全局置换宇称守恒的影响，发展了一套通用的LLSs和LLCs构造方法。

Result: 发现长寿命子空间包含2^N−1个独立非平凡算符（N为亚甲基数）；在非手性分子中因宇称守恒最多可访问2^N−2个，在手性分子中可达2^N−1个；并以N=2, 3, 4为例具体展示了该构造方法。

Conclusion: LLSs和LLCs是Redfield弛豫理论下自然出现的弛豫保护模式，其结构由分子内偶极弛豫主导的零本征态决定，手性影响其可观测算符的数量，该理论框架为设计长寿命核自旋态提供了普适指导。

Abstract: Delocalized long-lived states (LLSs) and collective zero-quantum long-lived coherences (LLCs) in aliphatic chains provide a promising route for preserving nuclear spin order with lifetimes that exceed the conventional $T_1$ and $T_2$ relaxation times, respectively. Their extended lifetimes make them attractive for applications including hyperpolarization storage, ligand-observed drug screening based on the loss of longevity upon binding to a target protein, and quantum information processing exploiting the collective properties of many-body spin systems. Although LLSs and LLCs have been observed experimentally in methylene networks, their origin and general structure in chains of arbitrary length have remained unclear. Here we show that these relaxation-protected modes follow directly from Redfield relaxation theory. Specifically, we construct the long-lived subspace, i.e., the zero-eigenvalue subspace of the relaxation superoperator associated with the dominant intra-pair dipole--dipole relaxation mechanism. The long-lived subspace contains $2^N-1$ independent non-trivial operators, which excludes the identity operator, where $N>1$ is the number of $-\mathrm{CH}_2-$ groups in the chain. In achiral molecules, conservation of the global intra-pair permutation parity restricts experimental access to at most $2^N-2$ of these operators, whereas in chiral molecules this parity is not conserved, making up to $2^N-1$ long-lived operators accessible. We further develop a general framework for constructing both LLSs and LLCs in aliphatic chains containing an arbitrary number of $-\mathrm{CH}_2-$ groups in achiral molecules, and illustrate the approach explicitly for chains with $N=2$, 3, and 4 methylene groups.

</details>


### [106] [Consequences of a dynamical no-signaling condition for classical-quantum interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07245)
*S. Camalet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种动力学无信号条件，用于约束经典-量子混合系统中的相互作用，确保不会出现超光速通信，并揭示了该条件下经典与量子自由度之间相互作用的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不引入额外数学结构的情况下描述混合系统，基于自然公理定义测量概率，并确保满足无信号条件以防止瞬时通信。

Method: 通过定义一种动力学广义的无信号条件，分析其对经典-量子混合动力学的影响，特别是在不同类型的混合状态下概率测度演化的凸线性性质。

Result: 发现对于具有经典轨迹且保持量子纯态的无信号混合动力学，经典自由度可影响量子部分，但反之不行；当允许所有混合态时，不存在不产生关联的真实经典-量子相互作用。

Conclusion: 动力学无信号条件等价于描述有限时间演化的概率测度变换的凸线性，限制了经典与量子系统之间的双向相互作用。

Abstract: Hybrid classical-quantum approaches are instrumental in numerous fields, from condensed matter physics to quantum information science. We recently proposed to describe hybrid systems starting from a set of natural axioms for measurement probabilities without adding any underlying mathematical structure. The so defined probability measures fulfill a no-signaling condition that ensures that instantaneous communication is impossible. We formulate here a dynamical generalization of this condition. It means that, for two independent systems, the outcome probabilities of a measurement made on one of them are not affected by a measurement performed earlier on the other. Analogous requirements are satisfied for usual classical and quantum bipartite systems and violating them would make faster-than-light signaling possible. The dynamical no-signaling condition has important consequences for classical-quantum interactions that depend on the hybrid approach used. For no-signaling hybrid dynamics with classical trajectories, the classical degrees of freedom can influence the quantum ones but the latter cannot react on the former. If pure states of quantum systems remain pure then the dynamical no-signaling condition implies the absence of classical reaction. When all hybrid states are allowed, there are no genuine classical-quantum interactions for no-signaling hybrid dynamics that do not generate correlations between the classical and the quantum degrees of freedom. In all these cases, the proposed condition is equivalent to the convex-linearity of the probability measure transformations describing finite-time evolutions.

</details>


### [107] [Theory of Deterministic Photon-Loss Subspaces for Quantum Interferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07253)
*Yadi Niu,Haoyang Zhang,Zihan Mo,Nuo Wang,Ying Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于奇异值分解的确定性光子损失子空间（DPLS）理论，揭示损耗系统中量子相干、退相干与光子数减少之间的相互作用，并可用于精确控制耗散系统中的量子干涉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解线性损耗光学系统中量子相干、退相干与光子数减少三者如何共同决定量子光演化的过程尚不清晰。

Method: 通过散射矩阵的奇异值分解（SVD），定义完全损耗和无损耗输入模式，建立确定性光子损失子空间（DPLS），并将输入态投影到这些正交子空间中分析其演化。

Result: 在每个DPLS中，损耗模式的光子完全耗尽，无损耗模式经历幺正演化；输出态为所有投影结果演化的统计混合。该理论成功解释了Anti-HOM干涉和量子态蒸馏，并实现了鲁棒的W态生成。

Conclusion: DPLS理论揭示了损耗系统中量子相干、退相干与光子数减少的内在关联，可通过工程化损耗来精确调控量子干涉，具有在量子态制备和量子信息处理中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum coherence, quantum decoherence, and photon number reduction are coexistent in linear lossy optical systems. However, how these three elements combine together to determine the evolution of the quantum light remains unclear. Here, based on singular value decomposition (SVD), we propose the theory of deterministic photon-loss subspace (DPLS) for quantum interferences in lossy systems. By performing an SVD of scattering matrices with singular values either 0 or 1, a series of completely lossy and lossless input modes are first defined. According to n_1,...,n_i photons in the first,..., i-th lossy modes, the Hilbert space of the input states can be decomposed into a set of orthogonal subspaces H_((n_1,...,n_i ))^in, i.e., deterministic photon-loss subspaces (DPLSs). When the concept of DPLS is established, the input state can be projected onto these DPLSs. In each DPLS, the photons in lossy modes will be completely dissipated, while those in lossless modes experience a unitary evolution. The output state is a statistical mixture of the evolved outcomes of all projections, since decoherence is a concurrent process. Then, based on the DPLS theory, we not only revisited Anti-HOM interference and the distillation of quantum states, but also demonstrate a robust W-state generation for various input states in a three-port lossy system with one-dimensional DPLSs. Through investigating the loss-induced subspace structure of the system, our general theory for analyzing quantum state evolution in lossy systems explicitly reveals the interplay among quantum coherence, quantum decoherence, and photon number reduction. By engineering the loss, the constructed DPLSs can be used to precisely control quantum interferences in dissipative systems, with potential applications in quantum state preparation, quantum logic operations, and other quantum information processes.

</details>


### [108] [The small effect of graviton-induced decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07257)
*Guri K. Buza,Marko Toroš*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了非相对论性量子物质系统与量化引力波相互作用的约化动力学，保留了引力耦合中二次项的完整依赖关系，揭示了其在不同引力子浴态下的退相干特性及相干保护现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力波与量子物质系统的相互作用机制，特别是二次耦合效应在量子层面的影响，避免线性近似掩盖关键量子特征。

Method: 采用Born-Markov和secular近似方法，微观推导出由多种态（真空、相干、数态、热态和压缩态）引力子浴诱导的Lindblad主方程，并通过显式重正化处理紫外发散的Lamb-shift哈密顿量。

Result: 发现物质希尔伯特空间分解为偶宇称和奇宇称子空间；相干引力子浴下退相干与真空相同，且一阶关联函数可重现经典引力波潮汐效应；真空态下低机械数态间存在相干保护，而数态和热态破坏该保护；压缩引力子浴则产生新的由 dressed 暗态构成的相干保护子空间。

Conclusion: 完整保留二次耦合能揭示引力相互作用中独特的量子效应，不同引力子浴态对量子相干性有显著差异影响，其中压缩态可实现新型相干保护，为探测引力波量子效应提供理论基础。

Abstract: We derive and investigate the reduced dynamics of a nonrelativistic quantum matter system interacting with quantized gravitational waves. Because gravitational radiation couples tidally to the mass quadrupole moment, we emphasize that the leading interaction is quadratic in the mechanical displacement. Rather than treating it as a complication to be removed by linearization, we retain this full quadratic dependence, which gives rise to two-phonon rather single-phonon processes typical in dipolar interactions. We clarify its relation to the effective linear coupling that often appears in optomechanical literature, but which conceals the delicate quantum signatures of the full quadratic coupling. Using the Born-Markov and secular approximations, we microscopically derive Lindblad master equations for the matter dynamics induced by graviton baths in multi-mode vacuum, coherent, number, thermal, and squeezed states. We treat the ultraviolet-divergent Lamb-shift Hamiltonian by an explicit renormalization procedure, with the mechanical frequency identified as the physical frequency. Remarkably, owing to the nature of the interaction, the matter Hilbert space decomposes into even- and odd-parity sectors for all bath states considered. For a coherent graviton bath, the decoherence is identical to that of the vacuum, while the bath one-point function produces a coherent-shift Hamiltonian that exactly reproduces the tidal interaction with a classical gravitational wave. For a vacuum graviton bath, we recover the coherence protection between the two lowest mechanical number states, while number and thermal graviton baths remove this protection and enhance decoherence, although the effect remains small for realistic parameters. By contrast, a squeezed graviton bath enlarges the coupled coherence structure, and gives rise to a novel, coherence-protected sector spanned by dressed dark states.

</details>


### [109] [Demonstration of an LLO CV-QKD system over 12 km of optical fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07277)
*Christiano M. S. Nascimento,Artur A. Matoso,Gustavo C. Amaral,Guilherme P. Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文实现了基于完全独立的发射和接收激光器的高斯连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD），在12公里光纤上验证了系统性能，取得了超过5 Mbps的保密密钥率，为城域光纤网络中的严格安全CV-QKD部署奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CV-QKD需传输本地振荡器参考信号，存在安全隐患且限制应用。本文旨在消除对共传本地振荡器的依赖，提升系统安全性。

Method: 采用完全独立的发射端和接收端激光器实现CV-QKD，使用逻辑帧（含约10^7个相干态）进行实验评估，每个逻辑帧包含十个子帧，每子帧约10^6个状态，并在渐近和有限码长两种条件下进行安全分析。

Result: 系统成功恢复信道参数，在渐近条件下实现5.11 Mbit/s的密钥率，在有限码长条件下实现4.67 Mbit/s，与理论预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需共传本地振荡器的CV-QKD在实际光纤链路中的可行性，为未来城域网中高安全、易集成的量子密钥分发提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) promises high rates and seamless integration with classical beams within a single optical fiber. Over the years, implementations have been performed by transmitting a local oscillator reference along with the quantum channel, opening security loopholes for eavesdroppers and limiting potential applications. Here, we report on a Gaussian CV-QKD implementation using fully independent transmitter and receiver lasers (local-oscillator sources) over a 12 km fiber spool. The system was experimentally evaluated using logical frames containing approximately $10^7$ coherent states, each composed of ten independently processed subframes of approximately $10^6$ states, and security was assessed in both asymptotic and finite-size regimes under a trusted-device model. The full-fledged classical post-processing is capable of recovering the channel parameters and extracting secret key rates of 5.11 Mbit/s in the asymptotic regime and 4.67 Mbit/s in the finite-size regime, showing good agreement with theoretical predictions. This work establishes the foundation for metropolitan fiber deployment of CV-QKD under strict security constraints.

</details>


### [110] [Flip-chip integrated superconducting qubits using electroplated bump bonds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07306)
*Yen-An Shih,Rebecca Gharibaan,Barka Khan,Dhananjay Joshi,Siddharth Singh,Martijn F. S. Zwanenburg,Eugene Y. Huang,Nataliia Zhurbina,Figen Yilmaz,Lukas Johannes Splitthoff,Srijit Goswami,Christian Kraglund Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电镀铟的三维transmon架构，用于可扩展的超导量子处理器和混合量子器件。该结构通过倒装芯片集成实现近似均等的电场分布，并保持在铟凸点界面的低参与度，表现出高达10^6的量子比特品质因数，展示了电镀铟技术与高相干性超导电路的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可扩展的超导量子处理器和混合量子器件，需要高效的三维集成方案。传统方法在界面损耗和材料兼容性方面存在挑战，因此亟需一种能够降低界面敏感性并支持多材料集成的新架构。

Method: 采用电镀铟技术构建三维transmon架构，利用倒装芯片键合使量子比特电场在两个基板间均匀分布，并通过系统研究共面波导谐振器来分析电镀过程中的损耗来源。

Result: flip-chip transmons使用电镀铟凸点后量子比特品质因数达到约10^6；通过谐振器研究发现，用于保证铟电接触的金层表面损耗是导致量子比特退相干的主要因素。

Conclusion: 电镀铟技术与高相干超导电路兼容，所提出的三维架构为未来混合量子器件的集成提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Flip-chip integration offers a promising route toward scalable superconducting quantum processors and hybrid semiconductor-superconductor quantum devices. We develop a three-dimensional transmon architecture using electroplated indium in which the qubit electric field is shared nearly equally between two bump-bonded substrates while maintaining low participation at the indium-bump interface. The resulting geometry is well suited for future hybrid qubits, enabling the integration of distinct material platforms while minimizing sensitivity to bump-interface loss. Using this platform, we evaluate electroplated indium interconnects for superconducting quantum circuits. Flip-chip transmons incorporating electroplated indium bumps exhibit qubit quality factors around $10^6$. In addition, a systematic study of coplanar-waveguide resonators is used to identify losses associated with the electroplating process. In particular, we find that surface losses associated with the gold-layer, used to enable good electric contact with the indium, is likely the primary contributor to the qubit decay rate. These results demonstrate the compatibility of electroplated indium technology with high-coherence superconducting circuits and establish a promising platform for three-dimensional hybrid quantum integration.

</details>


### [111] [QMIMO: Circuit Based Quantum MIMO Design with Variational Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07428)
*Sayeda Bipanchi Ahmed,Bikash K. Behera,Mandar Thatte,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于变分量子电路（VQC）的量子MIMO通信接收机，通过学习逆信道变换在噪声环境下恢复信息，虽平均误码率较高，但在复杂信道中表现更稳定，可作为经典方法的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子扩展的MIMO通信系统，在强耦合和复杂干扰场景下克服经典检测方法性能下降的问题。

Method: 将传统线性信道模型替换为参数化多量子比特酉变换，使用变分量子电路（VQC）接收机通过监督式变分优化学习近似逆信道变换以恢复信号。

Result: 在NISQ条件下评估显示，经典检测器在多数参数下误码率更低，但在特定信道配置中性能显著下降；VQC接收机误码率较高但性能更稳定，随信道复杂度增加波动较小。

Conclusion: 变分量子接收机并非经典方法的直接替代，而是一种在强耦合与复杂干扰通信场景中提供更高性能稳定性的互补方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates a quantum extension of classical Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (MIMO) communication in which the conventional linear channel model is replaced by a parameterized multi-qubit unitary transformation. Within this framework, interference is represented through coherent quantum interactions rather than additive signal coupling. To recover transmitted information, a Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) receiver is introduced that learns an approximate inverse channel transformation through supervised variational optimization. The proposed system is evaluated under realistic noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) conditions incorporating depolarizing noise, thermal relaxation, and measurement imperfections, and its performance is compared with that of standard classical detection methods. The results reveal a trade-off between the two approaches: classical detectors achieve substantially lower bit-error rates across much of the investigated parameter range but exhibit pronounced performance degradation for specific channel configurations, whereas the VQC receiver maintains a more uniform error profile as channel complexity increases, albeit at a higher average BER. These findings suggest that variational quantum receivers are not a direct replacement for classical detection methods, but rather a complementary approach that may offer increased performance stability in communication scenarios characterized by strong coupling and complex interference patterns.

</details>


### [112] [Designer Codes from GALA: Compact, Self-Dual, and Rate-1/2 QEC on Reconfigurable Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07431)
*Willers Yang,Casey Duckering,Arpit Dua*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GALA的新量子纠错码族，通过群作用提升和主动正交性设计，实现了高码率、低开销且硬件兼容的量子低密度奇偶校验码，适用于中性原子阵列系统，并在紧凑性和逻辑错误率方面显著优于已有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子低密度奇偶校验码在近期硬件上面临规模小、可重构性有限的问题，缺乏同时满足低开销、紧凑结构和硬件兼容性的实用化编码方案，因此需要一种可定制化设计、适配实际物理指令（如AOD移动调度）的新型码。

Method: 引入GALA码，基于乘积群G = H_k × C_m的群作用提升构造，其中非阿贝尔因子H_k提供主动正交性以实现高码率和高于检验权重的距离，阿贝尔因子C_m提供对称性以生成码自同构和简单的AOD移动调度，从而将硬件兼容性和逻辑操作能力作为设计输入而非事后验证。

Result: GALA码族包含多个已知Kasai码并简化了参数界限与逻辑操作；发现了紧凑自对偶码[[132,30,12]]（接近围长6，4-环少），逻辑错误率低于10^-8；girth-6的[[672,336,12]]码，周期6.76ms，逻辑错误率低于10^-10；突破率-1/2限制的[[1752,880,14]]和[[2232,1120,16]]码，距离严格大于检验权重，且所有码均比此前已知的硬件兼容码更小。

Conclusion: GALA码通过群结构设计实现了硬件兼容性与逻辑性能的协同优化，是首个将硬件约束直接嵌入构造过程的“按需设计”量子LDPC码，为中性原子平台上的实用化量子纠错提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: High rate quantum low-density parity-check codes on reconfigurable neutral-atom arrays can reduce the overhead of quantum error correction, but near-term devices support only hundreds of qubits with limited reconfigurability from a few crossed acousto-optic deflectors (AOD). A practical code must be compact in addition to low-overhead, with checks and logical gates mapping onto hardware-compatible physical instructions. We introduce the GALA codes, or Group-Action Lifts with Active orthogonality, that lifts over a product group $G = H_k \times C_m$ (or $H_k \ltimes C_m^k$). The small non-abelian factor $H_k$ supplies active orthogonality, reaching $1/2$ rate with above-weight distance, while the large abelian factor $C_m$ supplies symmetries that give code automorphisms and explicit, simple AOD move schedules. Hardware compatibility and logical capability thereby become customizable inputs to a code search rather than properties verified post-hoc, making GALA designer codes by construction. The GALA family contains several previously discovered rate-1/2 Kasai codes of Ref. [arXiv:2601.08824, arXiv:2604.16209] while exposing simpler parameter bounds, logical operations, and ZX-dual variants with AOD-compatible fold-transversal Clifford gates. Our search yields a compact self-dual $[[132, 30, 12]]$ with a small number of $4$-cycles (almost girth-6) and below $10^{-8}$ logical error rate (LER) for memory at $10^{-3}$ physical error rate, 3.1ms syndrome-extraction cycle and transversal Clifford gates; a girth-6, rate-$1/2$ $[[672, 336, 12]]$ with a 6.76ms cycle with below $10^{-10}$ LER (extrapolated) and rate-$1/2$ barrier-breaking $[[1752, 880, 14]]$ and $[[2232, 1120, 16]]$ with exactly certified distances greater than check weights and all smaller than previously known hardware compatible rate-1/2 codes.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Record Values Based Inaccuracy Measures with an Application to Testing Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06548)
*Santosh Kumar Chaudhary,Bhagwati Devi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于记录值的信息论不准确度量及其在对称性检验中的应用，推导了多种常见寿命分布下不同不准确度量的显式表达式，并提出了一种基于上下记录值不准确度差异的非参数对称性检验方法，通过Bootstrap方法获取检验统计量的零分布，模拟研究表明该方法在保持名义水平的同时对偏斜替代方案具有高功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了发展可用于检验分布对称性的新型非参数方法，利用记录值的不准确度量特性，特别是在缺乏先验分布假设的情况下实现有效的统计推断。

Method: 研究Kerridge不准确度、累积残余不准确度、累积过去不准确度及基于熵的不准确度在第n个上、下k-记录值分布中的表现；针对指数、帕累托、威布尔和均匀分布推导显式表达式；分析其随记录序n、参数k及分布参数的变化趋势；构造基于上下记录Kerridge不准确度之差的检验统计量，并采用强制对称的Bootstrap方法确定其零分布。

Result: 得到了各类不准确度量在常见分布下的解析表达式，发现其具有单调性特征；提出的对称性检验方法在模拟中能良好维持名义检验水平，并对偏态分布表现出高检验功效；在年最大气温数据上的实证分析验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 基于记录值的不准确度量可有效用于构建非参数对称性检验方法，所提检验具有良好统计性质和实际应用前景。

Abstract: This paper studies information-theoretic inaccuracy measures for record values and their application to testing symmetry. We study the Kerridge inaccuracy measure, cumulative residual inaccuracy, cumulative past inaccuracy, and extropy-based inaccuracy measures to the distributions of nth upper and lower k-record values. Explicit expressions are derived for several common lifetime distributions (exponential, Pareto, Weibull, and uniform), and the monotonic behaviour of these measures with respect to the record order n, the parameter k, and the distributional parameters is analysed. Building on the known characterisation that equality of upper and lower record-based inaccuracy implies symmetry, we develop a nonparametric goodness-of-fit test for symmetry. The test statistic is the difference between the estimated Kerridge inaccuracy measures for upper and lower records, and its null distribution is obtained by a bootstrap procedure that enforces symmetry. A simulation study demonstrates that the test maintains its nominal level for a variety of symmetric distributions and achieves high power against skewed alternatives, while a real-data illustration on annual maximum temperatures confirms its practical usefulness.

</details>


### [114] [Bootstrap validity in Bayesian semi-parametric models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06670)
*Magid Sabbagh,David A. Stephens*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在存在复杂干扰成分的情况下，使用估计函数方法进行半参数推断的贝叶斯推断框架。通过狄利克雷过程和贝叶斯自助法获得后验分布，并放松了常见的随机等度连续性假设，证明了后验分布渐近正态且集中在参数真值上，具有良好的频率特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在高维或复杂 nuisance 参数时，传统的贝叶斯半参数推断常依赖强假设（如随机等度连续性），限制了其适用性。本文旨在构建一个更宽松、实用的框架，以实现对低维目标参数的有效推断。

Method: 采用基于估计函数的半参数贝叶斯方法，利用狄利克雷过程和贝叶斯自助法构造非参数先验，并通过渐近分析研究后验分布的频率性质，特别放松了随机等度连续性的要求。

Result: 证明了所提出的后验分布在适当条件下是渐近正态的，并能以正确的速率集中于真实参数值；明确了实现良好频率性质所需的关键假设，并展示了放宽某些假设对结论的影响；并通过模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为在复杂模型中对低维参数进行贝叶斯推断提供了坚实的理论基础，兼具贝叶斯解释性和优良的频率覆盖性能，拓展了传统方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We discuss Bayesian inference on a low-dimensional targeted parameter in the presence of possibly highly complex nuisance components within the semi-parametric inference framework using an estimating function approach. We obtain a posterior distribution using non-parametric Bayesian methods through the Dirichlet process and the Bayesian bootstrap. We relax the commonly deployed notion of stochastic equicontinuity and develop a framework leading to posterior inference with good frequentist properties, specifically we demonstrate that the posterior distribution is asymptotically Normal and concentrates at the true value of the parameter. We emphasize the specific assumptions that are required to obtain these results, and how relaxing any of them alters the conclusions. We verify the analytical results in simulation.

</details>


### [115] [On equitable scoring functions and optimal forecasting behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06710)
*Robert J. Taggart,Nicholas Loveday*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了公平评分函数，提出了广义对角线评分框架，揭示了其在不同气候背景下可能导致相同预测分布产生不同最优预测的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解公平评分函数（如SEEPS）的性质及其在气象预报评估中的适用性，需要一个统一的理论框架来分析其诱导的最优预测行为。

Method: 引入广义对角线评分作为包含SEEPS、Gerrity等评分的广泛公平评分函数类，通过分析预测与气候累积分布函数的交叉点来刻画最优预测，并研究其在概率预报下的适当性和严格适当性。

Result: 发现广义对角线评分是适当的但非严格适当的，对远离交叉点的预测误设不敏感；最优单值和分类预测由预测与气候CDF的交叉点决定，且随气候背景变化而变化。

Conclusion: 尽管公平评分函数具有理论吸引力，但其诱导的最优预测依赖于气候背景，可能影响其在实际应用中的适宜性，需谨慎选择使用场景。

Abstract: Equitable scoring functions have a long history in meteorological forecast verification and have recently gained renewed prominence through the use of the Stable Equitable Error in Probability Space (SEEPS) score for evaluating precipitation forecasts from numerical and machine-learning weather prediction systems.
  This paper provides a systematic analysis of equitable scoring functions through the optimal forecasts they induce. We introduce the generalized diagonal score, which defines a broad class of equitable scoring functions that includes, up to equivalence, the SEEPS, Gerrity, Peirce, Barnston and diagonal scores. Within this framework, we show that optimal single-valued and categorical forecasts are characterized by crossing points between the predictive and climatological cumulative distribution functions. Moreover, we show that the generalized diagonal score, when evaluating predictive distributions, is proper but not strictly proper, and is insensitive to substantial forecast misspecification away from crossing points. The framework also yields scoring rules that are both proper and equitable for probabilistic forecasts with categorical outcomes.
  For single-valued and categorical forecasts, the crossing-point characterization shows that optimal forecasts can vary across climatologies even when the underlying predictive distribution is unchanged. Consequently, identical predictive distributions may lead to substantially different optimal forecasts under equitable scores, with important implications for assessing the suitability of such scores for any given application.

</details>


### [116] [Lambda-quantiles under the microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07122)
*Fabio Bellini,Felix-Benedikt Liebrich*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lambda-分位数，即用函数参数Λ替代常数概率水平λ的广义分位数，并探讨了非单调Λ情形下的性质与重构问题，提出了混合表示结果及风险测度的序协方差群概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了扩展经典分位数的概念并研究其在非单调函数参数下的性质，特别是在聚合和混合操作下的封闭性。

Method: 通过分析Lambda-分位数在不同分布族上的取值来重构函数参数Λ，并研究其弱上下半连续性和凸性等性质；引入有界变差条件以获得混合表示结果，并定义序协方差群进行深入分析。

Result: 证明了在适当假设下Lambda-分位数具有可识别性；获得了关于半连续性和凸性的几乎完整刻画；建立了有界变差情形下的混合表示定理；明确了序协方差群与Λ的组合不变群及测度保持变换群的关系。

Conclusion: Lambda-分位数在非单调Λ情况下仍具有良好性质，可通过混合表示简化参数复杂度，且其序协方差群揭示了深层对称结构。

Abstract: We study Lambda-quantiles, a generalisation of classical quantiles in which the constant probability level $λ\in [0,1]$ is replaced by a functional parameter $Λ\colon \mathbb{R} \to [0,1]$. We consider the general case of non-monotone $Λ$, which arises naturally if closure properties of the class of corresponding Lambda-quantiles with respect to inf-aggregation or with respect to mixtures are required. As preliminary results, we characterise finiteness, constancy, and what we call the attainment property known from classical quantiles. We then consider the problem of reconstructing $Λ$ from the values of $Λ$-quantiles on a suitable family of simple distributions, showing its identifiability under mild assumptions. Next, we substantially refine several results obtained in the literature on weak upper and lower semicontinuity and on the property of convexity of the level sets with respect to mixtures, obtaining in both cases almost complete characterisations without any monotonicity assumption. We then move to the case in which $Λ$ has bounded variation, which enables us to prove a mixture representation result: any such $Λ$-quantile can be rewritten as a Lambda-quantile with an increasing functional parameter, evaluated at a mixture of the original distribution with a fixed reference distribution at a fixed weight, thus reducing the complexity of the parameter from bounded variation to monotone. Finally, we introduce and study the notion of the ordinal covariance group of a risk measure, showing that in the case of a $Λ$-quantile it coincides with the compositional invariance group of $Λ$ and with a certain group of measure-preserving transformations of the signed measure associated with $Λ$.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Networks with Node Heterogeneity and Homophily](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07175)
*Binyan Jiang,Qiwei Yao,Xinyang Yu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动态网络中节点异质性和连接同质性建模的新框架，结合观测特征与潜在特征，解决了高维统计推断中的参数混淆问题，并通过归一化平方损失实现高效稳定估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和预测动态网络的演化，需要同时考虑节点异质性和由观测及潜在特征引起的链接同质性，但现有方法在高维设置下面临统计推断困难。

Method: 提出一种联合建模节点异质性与显式、隐式同质性效应的新模型，并设计一种新的归一化平方损失函数以实现高维场景下的高效稳定参数估计。

Result: 理论分析证明了所提估计方法的合理性，模拟实验和真实数据验证了其在预测未来网络结构方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为动态网络建模提供了更精细的工具，在兼顾统计保证的同时提升了对未来网络预测的能力。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is to model node heterogeneity and link homophily for dynamic networks. The proposed framework brings new insights on how networks evolve over time. It also provides more sophisticated tools for the prediction of future networks with statistical guarantees. The new model accounts for the link homophily associated with both observed traits and latent traits. The joint modeling of node heterogeneity and both observed and latent homophily effects also poses the significant challenge in statistical inference, resulted from the large number of confounding parameters in the model. To overcome this, we propose a novel normalized squared loss, paving the way for efficient and stable estimation of parameters in a high-dimensional setting. We provide a rigorous theoretical analysis of the estimation method, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive simulations and the illustration with some real-world network data.

</details>


### [118] [High-dimensional ridgeless least squares interpolation under spiked covariance structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07281)
*Zhijun Liu,Dandan Jiang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维岭最小二乘估计器在特征维度和样本量成比例增长时的样本外预测风险的渐近行为，提出了广义尖峰总体协方差模型，并揭示了插值预测中良性和灾难性过拟合的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在高维环境下，特别是当特征数与样本数相当时，岭最小二乘估计器的预测性能及其受总体协方差结构影响的方式。

Method: 采用广义尖峰总体协方差模型分析方法，考虑多个潜在因子的影响，同时允许尖峰特征值的数量有限或随样本量增加而增加，以及这些特征值可能以任意速率有界或发散。

Result: 理论框架建立了在最小矩条件下（仅需有限四阶矩）的精确预测风险极限，揭示了尖峰的数量、强度和几何结构如何共同影响双重下降现象。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了一个统一的理解框架，说明何时潜在协方差结构有助于或阻碍过参数化回归中的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper investigates the asymptotic behavior of the out-of-sample prediction risk of the high-dimensional ridgeless least-squares estimator when the feature dimension $p$ and the sample size $n$ grow proportionally. We consider a generalized spiked population covariance model with multiple latent factors, where the number of spiked eigenvalues may remain finite or increase with $n$, and the spiked eigenvalues may be bounded or diverge at arbitrary rates. Beyond characterizing the impact of covariance spectra, we reveal a new mechanism underlying benign overfitting: the prediction behavior of ridgeless interpolation is fundamentally governed by the alignment between the regression coefficient $\boldsymbolβ$ and the spiked eigenspaces of the population covariance matrix. In particular, we show that the signal energy distributed along latent spike directions determines whether interpolation leads to benign, tempered, or catastrophic overfitting. Our theoretical framework establishes sharp prediction risk limits under minimal moment conditions, requiring only finite fourth moments rather than Gaussianity. We characterize how the number, strength, and geometric structure of the spikes jointly influence the double-descent phenomenon. These results provide a unified understanding of when latent covariance structures facilitate or hinder generalization in overparameterized regression.

</details>


### [119] [Spectral Dependence of Convex Regularization: Fundamental Limits under Right-Rotationally Invariant Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07432)
*Baichen Tan,Audrey Yang,Cynthia Rush*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究了高维线性回归中凸正则化估计的极限，提出并证明了凸惩罚最小二乘估计器的风险下界，并刻画了该下界可达的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在右旋转不变设计矩阵下，凸正则化方法在高维线性回归中的基本性能极限。

Method: 通过分析带有ℓ2扰动的凸估计器在任意固定扰动强度下的渐近表现，结合近似消息传递算法（Bayes VAMP）的风险，推导出渐近风险的下界。

Result: 证明了凸惩罚最小二乘估计器的渐近风险被Bayes VAMP算法的风险所下界，并明确了该下界可达的条件；同时揭示了设计矩阵的奇异值分布对凸正则化性能限制的影响。

Conclusion: 设计矩阵的完整奇异值分布（而不仅仅是维度比或平均测量强度）决定了凸正则化的性能极限，且该极限可通过所提出的分析框架进行精确刻画。

Abstract: We study the fundamental limits of convex-regularized estimation in high-dimensional linear regression with right-rotationally invariant design matrices. We show that the asymptotic risks of convex-penalized least squares estimators are lower bounded by the risk of an approximate message passing algorithm known as Bayes VAMP, and we further characterize when the lower bound is attainable. As a technical ingredient in the proof of our lower bound theorem, we characterize the asymptotic performance of the $\ell_2$-perturbed convex estimator for every fixed perturbation strength $λ>0$. This closes a gap in the literature, in which $λ$ was required to be sufficiently large or restrictions were imposed on the class of convex estimators.
  The benefit of our approach is that we can conduct a direct analysis of the spectrum's impact on the lower bound in the high-dimensional limit. In particular, we can show that the lower bound is monotone in an ordering on the limiting spectral distributions of the design matrix. These results isolate how the full singular-value distribution of the design---not merely the aspect ratio or average measurement strength---governs the limitations of convex regularization.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Mean field error estimate of the random batch method for vortex blob dynamics for the 2D Navier--Stokes Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06533)
*Zhenyu Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了用于二维Navier-Stokes方程涡量形式的随机批次涡旋块方法，通过减少计算复杂度实现了高效的数值模拟，并给出了关于误差估计的定量规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低传统涡旋块方法在处理2D Navier-Stokes方程时的高计算复杂度（O(N^2)），提高大规模粒子系统模拟的效率。

Method: 结合随机批次方法与涡旋块法，利用局部耦合辅助划分、对称律比较和Fisher信息耗散控制随机批次误差；通过利用Biot-Savart核的奇性和无散特性估计平均场波动。

Result: 在有限时间区间上证明了归一化相对熵界为O(ε^(-4)τ^2 + N^(-1))，且当N→∞和ε^(-2)τ→0时，固定粒子边缘收敛到正则化涡量解的张量积。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了计算成本，同时保持了对光滑、严格正初值涡量的良好收敛性，为大尺度流体模拟提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We propose and analyze the random batch vortex blob method for the 2D Navier--Stokes equation in vorticity form on the whole plane. The vortex blob method is based on an interacting particle system of $N$ particles with computational complexity of $O(N^2)$, which is reduced to $O(N)$ by the random batch method \cite{JinLiLiu2020}. Our main result is a quantitative law level mean field error estimate whose dependence on the blob radius remains algebraic. We treat the two main error mechanisms separately. The random batch error is controlled through a locally coupled auxiliary partition, a symmetric law comparison, and Fisher-information dissipation. The mean field fluctuation is estimated by exploiting the oddness and divergence-free structure of the Biot--Savart kernel. For smooth, strictly positive initial vorticity, we prove on every finite time interval a normalized relative-entropy bound of order $ O\!\left(\varepsilon^{-4}τ^2+N^{-1} \right), $ with constants independent of $N$, $τ$, and $varepsilon$. Here $τ$ is the batch refreshing interval and $\varepsilon$ is the blob radius. As a consequence, the fixed-particle marginals converge strongly in $L^1$ to tensor products of the regularized vorticity solution when $N\to\infty$ and $\varepsilon^{-2}τ\to 0$.

</details>


### [121] [The error estimate of entropy-stable discontinuous Galerkin methods for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06642)
*Xu-Kun Chen,Yong Liu,Chi-Wang Shu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了半离散熵稳定间断Galerkin方法在一般非结构网格上对双曲守恒律的先验误差估计，填补了此前该类高阶格式收敛性理论的空白，并推广至新近提出的无振荡熵稳定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有构造满足熵不等式的高阶DG方法的统一框架，但其严格的收敛性理论仍缺失，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于节点层面的有限差分型相容-稳定性分析，在多项式重构假设和L^∞先验有界条件下，对熵稳定DG方法建立a priori误差估计，并将其推广至熵稳定无振荡DG方法。

Result: 证明了在基于积分准则的范数下具有O(h^k)的误差估计，该范数在有限维重构空间上等价于分片L^2范数；理论结果适用于标量方程与方程组，并表明ESOFDG中的额外阻尼项不降低收敛阶；数值实验显示实际收敛率可能比理论预测高出约半阶。

Conclusion: 本文为熵稳定DG方法建立了严格的收敛性理论基础，验证了其在一般非结构网格上的最优收敛性，并确认了新近提出的ESOFDG方法在保持高阶精度方面的有效性。

Abstract: Entropy inequalities are fundamental to the well-posedness of hyperbolic conservation laws, providing the essential criterion for selecting the physically admissible solution among infinitely many weak solutions. Chen and Shu [J. Comput. Phys. 345 (2017)] proposed a unified framework for constructing high-order discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods that satisfy entropy inequalities for any given entropy via specific numerical quadrature; however, their accompanying error analysis was limited to the truncation error level, leaving a critical gap in the rigorous convergence theory for these entropy-stable schemes. This paper closes that gap by establishing rigorous a priori error estimates for semi-discrete entropy-stable DG (ESDG) methods on general unstructured meshes for hyperbolic conservation laws. The analysis applies to both scalar equations and systems, and is built upon a finite-difference-type consistency-stability argument carried out directly at the nodal level. Under a polynomial-reconstruction hypothesis and an $L^\infty$ a priori bound, we prove an $O(h^k)$ error estimate in a quadrature-based norm, which is equivalent to the broken $L^2$ norm on the finite-dimensional reconstruction space. We further extend this framework to the entropy-stable oscillation-free DG (ESOFDG) method introduced by Liu, Lu, and Shu [SIAM J. Sci. Comput. 46 (2024)], demonstrating that the additional damping terms do not degrade the convergence order. Numerical experiments suggest that the observed convergence rates may exceed the theoretical prediction by up to half an order.

</details>


### [122] [CT-PIKAN: Coordinate-Transformed Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Network with Autograd-Based Metric Evaluation for Solving PDEs in Curvilinear Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06660)
*Mohammad E. Heravifard,Kazem Hejranfar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于坐标变换的物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（CT-PIKAN），用于在任意形状域上求解偏微分方程，利用自动微分避免手动推导，提升几何适应性与实现灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络主要适用于笛卡尔域，难以处理曲线坐标域带来的几何复杂性，限制了其在复杂几何问题中的应用。

Method: 引入坐标变换将物理域映射到规则计算域，并通过自动微分直接从坐标映射计算雅可比矩阵、度量张量和变换后的微分算子，在无需手动推导的情况下构建物理信息损失函数。

Result: 在极坐标和波动曲线坐标下的Poisson、热传导和对流方程等典型椭圆、抛物和双曲型问题上验证了CT-PIKAN的有效性，结果显示其能高效准确地求解复杂域上的PDE。

Conclusion: CT-PIKAN为将微分几何与物理信息神经网络结合提供了一个通用且可扩展的框架，显著增强了Kolmogorov-Arnold网络在复杂几何域上的适用性与精度。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks have recently emerged as an effective class of neural solvers for partial differential equations, combining the expressive power of spline-based Kolmogorov-Arnold representations with physics-informed learning. However, existing PIKAN formulations are primarily developed for Cartesian domains and cannot naturally accommodate the geometric complexity introduced by curvilinear domains. In this work, we propose Coordinate-Transformed Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (CT-PIKAN), a geometry-aware framework for solving PDEs on arbitrarily shaped domains through coordinate transformation. A smooth mapping transforms the physical domain into a regular computational domain, while the transformed governing equations are enforced directly within the physics-informed loss. Unlike conventional transformed PINN approaches that require manually derived metric coefficients, CT-PIKAN employs automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobians, metric tensors, and transformed differential operators directly from the coordinate mapping, eliminating analytical derivations and improving implementation flexibility. To establish the proposed framework, a data-free B-spline-based PIKAN is first constructed and validated on the two-dimensional advection equation. The CT-PIKAN methodology is subsequently assessed on representative elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic benchmark problems, including the Poisson, heat, and advection equations formulated in polar and wavy curvilinear coordinates. The proposed framework provides a general and extensible methodology for integrating differential geometry with physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, enabling efficient and accurate PDE solutions on complex domains.

</details>


### [123] [Multivariate integration and approximation in weighted Sobolev spaces of low fractional smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06685)
*Mou Cai,Takashi Goda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了半周期余弦空间与加权无锚Sobolev空间在分数阶光滑性下的范数等价性，建立了显式的Slobodeckij型半范数表示，并揭示了分数阶正则性对边界约束和周期结构的影响，同时证明了当光滑性参数趋近整数时的极限行为，从而将已有的拟蒙特卡罗方法误差界和可处理性结果推广到新的分数阶Sobolev空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更精确地处理非周期函数的多变量积分与逼近问题，需要将已有基于整数阶光滑性的半周期余弦空间与Sobolev空间的范数等价性推广到分数阶光滑情形，以增强函数空间的灵活性和适用范围。

Method: 通过引入基于Slobodeckij型半范数的显式表示，证明半周期余弦空间与相应分数阶加权无锚Sobolev空间之间的范数等价性，并分析光滑性参数趋于整数时的极限行为。

Result: 建立了半周期余弦空间与分数阶（至2阶）加权无锚Sobolev空间之间的范数等价性；给出了Sobolev范数的显式表达形式，阐明了分数阶正则性如何影响边界条件与周期结构的存在性；并证明了当光滑性趋于整数时，分数阶空间连续过渡到经典整数阶Sobolev空间。

Conclusion: 该等价性使得可以将在半周期余弦空间中获得的近最优积分误差界和可处理性结果迁移至新引入的分数阶Sobolev空间，拓展了拟蒙特卡罗方法的理论基础与应用范围。

Abstract: The weighted half-period cosine space has often been employed in the theory of quasi-Monte Carlo methods for multivariate integration and approximation of non-periodic functions. For integer-order smoothness, its norm equivalence to certain weighted unanchored Sobolev spaces has been established in the literature. In this work, we extend this equivalence to fractional-order smoothness up to $2$. By introducing an explicit representation via Slobodeckij-type seminorms, we prove a norm equivalence between the half-period cosine spaces and the corresponding weighted unanchored Sobolev spaces. Our Sobolev norm representation clarifies how the fractional regularity dictates the presence or absence of boundary constraints and (non-)periodic structures. Furthermore, we investigate the limiting behavior of these fractional spaces as the smoothness parameter approaches integer boundaries, establishing a continuous bridge to the classical integer-order Sobolev spaces. These equivalence results enable us to transfer the near-optimal error bounds and tractability results for multivariate integration and function approximation from the half-period cosine settings to our newly introduced fractional Sobolev spaces.

</details>


### [124] [Mesh-Based Filtering to Alleviate Time-Step Restrictions in Runge--Kutta Discontinuous Galerkin Methods in Spherical-polar Coordinates: Application to the Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07345)
*Joseph Hunter,Eirik Endeve,Yulong Xing*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于网格的滤波方法，用于缓解在球极坐标系中显式龙格-库塔间断伽辽金（RKDG）方法中的严重时间步长限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球极坐标系中，由于坐标线在奇点附近收敛导致极端单元各向异性，使得RKDG方法面临严重的时间步长限制，影响计算效率。

Method: 设计一种网格滤波器，构建一个消除各向异性的辅助合并网格，并将滤波作为s阶段RK时间积分器中的后处理步骤实现，从而允许在原始逻辑笛卡尔网格上使用更大的时间步长。

Result: 理论分析证明滤波后的RKDG方法等价于在非均匀合并网格上的RKDG离散化，继承其精度和稳定性；二维和三维数值实验表明该方法能显著增大稳定时间步长，有效加速模拟。

Conclusion: 所提出的网格滤波方法可无缝集成到现有结构化网格DG框架中，在保持精度的同时显著提升球极坐标下RKDG模拟的稳定性与效率。

Abstract: We propose a mesh-based filtering approach to alleviate the severe timestep restrictions arising in explicit Runge--Kutta discontinuous Galerkin (RKDG) methods formulated in spherical-polar coordinates. The filter enables stable evolution on the original logically Cartesian mesh while using larger time steps associated with an auxiliary merged mesh constructed to eliminate the extreme cell anisotropies produced by converging coordinate lines near coordinate singularities. The filter is implemented as a sequence of post-processing operations applied within an $s$-stage RK time integrator, making it straightforward to incorporate into existing structured-mesh DG frameworks. We analyze the filter in one spatial dimension and prove that the filtered RKDG method is equivalent to evolving the RKDG discretization on a nonuniform mesh obtained by merging selected elements of the underlying uniform mesh. This equivalence implies that the filtered method inherits the accuracy and stability properties of the corresponding RKDG discretization on the merged mesh. We apply the mesh-based filter to an existing RKDG method for the Euler equations in spherical-polar coordinates and demonstrate, through selected two- and three-dimensional examples, its effectiveness in accelerating simulations through significantly larger stable timesteps.

</details>


### [125] [Optimal Neural Network Approximation via Empirical Least Squares with Deterministic Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06687)
*Xinliang Liu,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性化ReLU^k神经网络的离散残差最小二乘逼近方法，用于求解球面上的椭圆型谱方程，并在参数点反极对拟均匀分布和配置点足够多的条件下，建立了H^β范数下的收敛误差估计，关键工具是针对该网络空间的Bernstein型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为椭圆型谱方程在高维球面上的数值求解提供一种基于神经网络的高效且理论严谨的离散最小二乘方法，克服传统方法在高维或复杂几何下的局限性。

Method: 利用线性化ReLU^k神经网络构造逼近函数空间，在给定网络参数集和配置点下，通过最小化离散残差进行逼近；结合谱算子性质、函数空间嵌入关系以及新建立的关于网络空间的Bernstein不等式进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了当k > (d-1)/2 + β，且参数集反极对拟均匀、配置点数m ≳ n时，逼近解在H^β范数下的误差以n^(-r/d)速率收敛，其右端项f在Sobolev或Besov空间中有相应正则性；同时给出了独立同分布均匀配置点下的高概率残差估计。

Conclusion: 该方法为使用神经网络求解椭圆型偏微分方程提供了坚实的理论基础，Bernstein不等式是分析此类线性化网络空间逼近能力的关键工具，结果表明该方法具有良好的收敛性和适应性。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theory of discrete residual least-squares approximation for elliptic spectral equations $\mathfrak L_βu=f$ using linearized ReLU$^k$ neural networks on the sphere, where $\mathfrak L_β$ is a positive elliptic spectral multiplier of order $β$. Given a parameter set $Θ_n=\{θ_{j}^*\}_{j=1}^n\subset\mathbb S^d$, we approximate $u$ in the linearized network space $L_n^k(Θ_n)$ by the discrete residual on the collocation points $\{η_i^*\}_{i=1}^m$ \begin{equation*} u_{n,m}\in\arg\min_{v_n\in L_n^k(Θ_n)}\frac1m\sum_{i=1}^m\left(f(η_i^*)-\mathfrak L_βv_n(η_i^*)\right)^2. \end{equation*} With $k>\frac{d-1}{2}+β$, for antipodally quasi-uniform network parameter sets and any quasi-uniform collocation points with $m\gtrsim n$, we prove that \begin{equation*} \|u-u_{n,m}\|_{\mathcal H^β(\mathbb S^d)}\eqsim\|f-\mathfrak L_βu_{n,m}\|_{\mathcal L^2(\mathbb S^d)}\lesssim n^{-\frac{r}{d}} \begin{cases} \|f\|_{\mathcal W^{r,p}(\mathbb S^d)},&\frac{d}{p}<r\leq \frac{d}{2},~p>2,\\ \|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(\mathbb S^d)},&r>\frac{d}{2}. \end{cases} \end{equation*} We also establish a high-probability residual estimate, up to a logarithmic factor and an arbitrarily small smoothness loss, for i.i.d.\ uniformly distributed collocation points.
  The key analytical ingredient is a Bernstein inequality for linearized ReLU$^k$ network spaces. If $\underline h$ denotes the antipodal separation distance of the network parameters, then \begin{equation*} \|v_n\|_{\mathcal H^r(\mathbb S^d)}\lesssim\underline h^{-(r-s)}\|v_n\|_{\mathcal H^s(\mathbb S^d)},\qquad 0\leq s<r<k+\tfrac12. \end{equation*}

</details>


### [126] [Weak Adversarial Neural Pushforward Method for Boltzmann Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06823)
*Jenia Fardousi Koly,Andrew Qing He,Wei Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于弱形式的神经网络推前方法，用于求解时变Boltzmann方程，通过可逆神经推前映射生成服从Boltzmann分布的样本，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在求解高维时变Boltzmann方程时面临计算复杂度高和采样困难的问题，需要一种高效且可扩展的数值方法。

Method: 提出了一种弱形式的碰撞算子表述，并利用可逆神经网络推前映射生成符合Boltzmann方程描述分布的样本；通过强制执行Boltzmann方程的弱形式来训练推前映射。

Result: 数值结果表明该方法能够有效逼近Boltzmann方程的解，并实现了高效的样本生成。

Conclusion: 所提方法为求解高维时变Boltzmann方程提供了一种可行且有效的基于学习的框架，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend a weak adversary neural network pushforward method for solving time dependent Boltzmann equation and a weak formulation of the collision operator is proposed where an invertible neural pushforward mapping is used to generating samples given by the distribution governed by the Boltzmann equation. The training of the pushforward mapping is learnt by enforcing the weak form of the Boltzmann equation. Numerical results have demonstrated the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [127] [On Generalized Barron Spaces for Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06843)
*Shuai Lu,Leqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于浅层神经网络的广义Barron空间 $ B_σ^{\varphi} $，适用于具有特定平滑性质的通用激活函数，并通过参数测度上的加权积分范数定义其平滑性。该空间可嵌入到Sobolev空间中，且涵盖了大多数传统函数空间。作为应用，文章推导了逼近速率并建立了正则化数值微分的误差界，数值实验验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一和推广适用于不同激活函数的浅层神经网络函数空间理论，克服经典Barron空间对ReLU等特定激活函数的限制，引入更具一般性的函数空间框架。

Method: 通过在神经网络参数测度上引入由 $ \varphi $ 加权的积分范数来定义广义Barron空间 $ B_σ^{\varphi} $，分析其与Sobolev空间及其他函数空间的关系，并在一定假设下证明连续嵌入性，进一步推导逼近率和误差界。

Result: 建立了广义Barron空间 $ B_σ^{\varphi} $ 的理论框架，证明其可连续嵌入Sobolev空间，统一了多种传统函数空间；获得了在该空间中的逼近速率和正则化数值微分的误差上界；数值示例显示该方法能构造不同平滑程度的神经网络。

Conclusion: 广义Barron空间为浅层神经网络提供了一个灵活且统一的函数空间框架，适用于广泛的激活函数，并支持理论分析与实际应用中的误差估计和逼近性能分析。

Abstract: Classical Barron spaces are function spaces specifically designed for shallow neural networks mostly with ReLU, $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$ (RePU) or Lipschitz continuous activation functions. In the present work, we introduce a generalized Barron space \( B_σ^{\varphi} \) for shallow neural networks with a generic activation function possessing certain smoothness properties. The subscript \( σ\) denotes the activation function, while the superscript \( \varphi \) controls the smoothness of the generalized Barron spaces, defined via a \( \varphi \)-weighted integral norm imposed on neural network parameter measures. Under certain assumptions on \( \varphi \) and \( σ\), we show that \( B_σ^{\varphi} \) can be continuously embedded into Sobolev spaces. We also explore the relationships among various function spaces for shallow neural networks, demonstrating that our definition encompasses most conventional ones. As applications of the proposed generalized Barron spaces, we derive approximation rates within these spaces and establish error bounds for numerical differentiation with regularization penalized by the newly introduced generalized Barron norm. Numerical examples confirm that the proposed spaces allow the construction of neural networks with varying degrees of smoothness while using the same activation function.

</details>


### [128] [Iterative thresholding low-rank time integration for high-dimensional problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06905)
*Markus Bachmayr,Tianyu Jin,Polina Sachsenmaier,Federico Vismara*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层张量近似的高维线性薛定谔型问题的时间积分方法，通过软阈值张量的迭代优化在误差和近似秩之间取得平衡，并通过耦合振子的数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决高维线性薛定谔型问题在时间积分过程中计算复杂度高的问题，需要一种既能控制误差又能保持较低计算成本的数值方法。

Method: 采用分层张量近似方法，结合带有软阈值处理的迭代细化方案，实现对高维问题的有效逼近与时间推进。

Result: 该方法能够在控制误差的同时有效限制张量近似秩的增长，数值实验表明其在耦合振子系统中具有良好的实际性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为高维线性薛定谔型问题提供了一种高效且可扩展的时间积分策略，适用于复杂量子系统的模拟。

Abstract: This work analyzes a method for time integration of high-dimensional linear Schrödinger-type problems based on hierarchical tensor approximations. In particular, this method provides a balance between error bounds and associated approximation ranks, using a scheme for iterative refinement with soft thresholding of tensors. The practical performance of the method is illustrated by numerical tests on coupled oscillators.

</details>


### [129] [A model and a finite element approximation of the mixed-dimensionality diffusion problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06976)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了有效模拟三维扩散固体与嵌入的一维扩散纤维之间的耦合扩散行为，需要建立一个能够处理非共形网格的数学模型和数值方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于能量最小化和耦合约束的变分形式的鞍点问题，并采用有限元方法进行离散化，允许非共形网格。

Abstract: We present the formulation of a boundary value problem that models the coupled behavior of a three-dimensional diffusive solid with one-dimensional diffusive fibers embedded inside it. We introduce a variational statement of the problem that identifies the linked diffusive fields as energy minimizers under a coupling constraint. This saddle-point problem is proved to be well posed. Then, we introduce a finite element discretization of the proposed boundary value problem, and we prove the convergence of the finite element solution to the exact one. The most significant feature of this approximation is that the meshes of the bodies need not be conforming. Numerical examples confirm the theoretical results

</details>


### [130] [Generalized high-order minimization-based polynomial corrections on unfitted spectral elements for the Poisson problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06999)
*Mirco Ciallella,Jens Visbech*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部最小化问题的多项式修正新方法，用于改进嵌入边界方法在复杂几何域中的精度，实现了对Neumann和Robin边界条件的高阶精度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高阶有限元方法在处理复杂曲面域时面临网格生成困难，而嵌入方法虽避免了完全网格生成但易受几何误差影响导致精度下降。因此需要一种能提高嵌入边界方法一致性的新策略。

Method: 通过求解一个带约束的局部最小化问题，构造一类新的多项式修正方法，推广了SBM和ROD方法，并针对不同边界条件设计了四种变分形式，可在每一点独立应用而不必求解完整的ROD线性系统。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法相比原始SBM具有更好的系统条件性，且在处理Poisson问题时对所有边界条件均能达到高阶收敛精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的最小化框架为嵌入边界方法提供了一个灵活、高效且高阶精确的修正方案，特别适用于复杂几何下的偏微分方程求解。

Abstract: Higher-order finite element methods are effective for solving partial differential equations. However, applying them in complex curved domains is often difficult due to challenges in creating high-quality curvilinear meshes. Unfitted, or embedded, methods provide a valid alternative by avoiding complete mesh generation and curved element integration, but their accuracy can suffer from geometric errors introduced during embedding. In this work, we explore a new family of polynomial corrections obtained by solving a local minimization problem to improve the consistency of embedded boundary methods. This approach generalizes existing techniques such as the shifted boundary method (SBM) and the reconstruction for off-site data (ROD) method. Unlike the SBM, which depends on truncated Taylor expansions, the proposed family of polynomial corrections is derived from a constrained minimization problem, similar to the ROD method. We demonstrate that this approach yields better system conditioning than the original SBM and provides a flexible framework that can be applied pointwise, without solving the full ROD linear system for each boundary element. This paper presents four formulations based on different functionals and extends the family of minimization-based polynomial corrections to handle Neumann and Robin boundary conditions, demonstrating that an elegant formulation is possible in this context. Several numerical experiments for the Poisson problem are presented to show that the generalized polynomial corrections achieve high-order accuracy across all boundary conditions.

</details>


### [131] [A High-Order Surface Finite Element Method Based on Intersections with Background Tetrahedral Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07000)
*Zibo Zhao,Zuoqiang Shi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于三维隐式定义光滑闭曲面上椭圆方程的高阶表面有限元方法，通过背景四面体网格与曲面的交集构造离散空间，并利用水平集局部参数化将有限元空间提升到精确曲面，结合了表面FEM与非适配/迹方法的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表面有限元方法依赖于预设的曲面三角剖分，难以处理复杂或隐式定义的几何形状；本文旨在发展一种适用于隐式曲面的高阶数值方法，避免显式网格生成。

Method: 在包围隐式曲面的背景四面体网格上识别被切割的单元，形成活动四面体壳；根据每个被切割四面体与零水平集的交点模式生成三角形或四边形表面片；在分段平面切割曲面上定义有限元空间，再通过基于水平集的局部参数化将其提升至精确曲面。

Result: 证明了局部提升映射在公共面上逐点一致并可组合成全局同胚；推导出切向量、Gram矩阵、质量与刚度积分的显式公式；建立了提升映射在断裂Sobolev范数下的稳定性和高阶导数估计，进而得到Cea型能量范数误差界。

Conclusion: 该方法成功融合表面FEM的保形性与非适配方法的几何灵活性，为隐式曲面上的偏微分方程求解提供了一种高阶、稳定的数值方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a high-order surface finite element method for elliptic equations posed on a smooth closed surface implicitly defined as the zero level set of a function in three dimensions. In contrast to classical surface finite element methods that start from a prescribed triangulation of the surface, the proposed method constructs the discrete surface space from the intersections between the exact surface and an ambient tetrahedral mesh. More precisely, an active tetrahedral shell is generated around the implicit surface, and each cut tetrahedron contributes either a triangular or a quadrilateral surface patch according to its intersection pattern with the zero level set. The finite element space is first defined on the resulting piecewise planar cut surface and is then lifted to the exact surface by local level-set-based parameterizations. The resulting method combines features of surface FEM and unfitted/trace methods. Like surface FEM, it produces a conforming finite element space on the exact surface after lifting; however, the geometry and finite element space are induced by intersections with background tetrahedra like unfitted/trace methods. We prove that the local lifting maps agree pointwise across common faces and assemble into a global homeomorphism. We also derive explicit formulas for tangent vectors, Gram matrices, mass and stiffness integrals, and prove stability and high-order derivative estimates for the lifting maps. The latter estimates are formulated in broken Sobolev norms and lead to a Cea-type energy-norm error bound for the proposed high-order lifted surface finite element method.

</details>


### [132] [Extended Walk-on-Spheres Algorithm for Linear and Nonlinear Elliptic Problems of Divergence-type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07017)
*Iulian Cîmpean,Andreea Grecu,Arghir Zarnescu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用框架，利用经典的狄利克雷拉普拉斯算子和调和延拓作为基础模块，扩展了Walk-on-Spheres算法的应用范围，以高效求解多种椭圆型偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Walk-on-Spheres算法原本仅适用于具有显式概率退出律的算子，难以推广到变系数和非线性椭圆算子，本文旨在突破这一限制。

Method: 使用经典的Dirichlet Laplacian和调和延拓作为通用构建块，通过Walk-on-Spheres预计算一个可重用的数值算子工具箱，用于近似逆算子及其梯度，并将任意Dirichlet边值问题转化为有限维代数系统进行求解。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理非对称、各向异性线性椭圆方程以及半线性和拟线性问题，数值实验表明其具有良好的灵活性和计算效率，并可估计Green积分算子和椭圆测度算子。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架显著扩展了Walk-on-Spheres方法的适用范围，为求解广泛类型的椭圆型PDE提供了高效、统一的蒙特卡洛解决方案。

Abstract: The Walk-on-Spheres algorithm, introduced by M. E. Muller in 1956, is a well known Monte Carlo method that leverages Brownian exit distributions from spheres to solve the Laplace equation with Dirichlet boundary conditions. Its mesh-free nature, robustness on complex geometries, favorable scaling with dimension, and intrinsic parallelism distinguish it from mesh-based solvers. However, its efficient applicability has been essentially limited to operators that admit explicit probabilistic exit laws, excluding most variable-coefficient and nonlinear elliptic operators. We propose a general framework that aims to overcome this limitation by using the classical Dirichlet Laplacian and harmonic extension as universal building blocks. Rather than seeking a custom stochastic representation for each operator, we employ Walk-on-Spheres to precompute a reusable numerical operator toolbox that approximates the inverse Dirichlet Laplacian, the harmonic extension operator, and their gradients. These precomputed operators are then used to represent candidate solutions and to transform arbitrary Dirichlet boundary value problems into finite-dimensional algebraic system for an unknown source term. Solving the resulting algebraic system and substituting back yields an approximate solution to the original PDE. Even more, for a general linear second order elliptic operator, the above mentioned precomputed toolbox can be directly used to obtain not just an approximation of a certain solution of the corresponding generalized Dirichlet problem, but an estimator of both the Green's integral operator and the elliptic measure operator. Numerical experiments on a range of benchmarks, including non-symmetric and anisotropic linear elliptic equations, semilinear and quasilinear problems, demonstrate the method's flexibility and efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [A unified analysis of maximum-norm estimates for a class of HDG methods for parabolic problem in polyhedral domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07096)
*Huangxin Chen,Haitao Leng,Weifeng Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类用于非凸多边形和多面体区域抛物型问题的半离散混合间断Galerkin（HDG）方法，建立了最大范数稳定性的一般性分析框架，并克服了数值通量处理和格式非对称带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非凸区域中抛物方程的HDG方法，缺乏统一的最大范数稳定性分析框架，尤其在混合格式下面临通量变量与非对称性带来的技术困难。

Method: 通过发展局部能量误差估计和正则化Green函数的能量估计，构建统一的稳定性分析框架，避免使用仅适用于单纯形网格的提升算子。

Result: 证明了半离散格式定义的半群及其解的最大范数稳定性，导出了半离散解在L^∞((0,T);L^p(Ω))范数下的最大正则性，并将最大范数误差估计归约到相应椭圆问题和L²正交投影的估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的分析工具具有足够普适性，可推广至更广泛的基于混合格式的抛物方程数值方法的最大范数稳定性研究，且结果适用于多边形/多面体网格（不含混合方法）。

Abstract: This paper studies a general class of semi-discrete hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin (HDG) methods, including mixed methods, for parabolic problems in nonconvex polygonal and polyhedral domains. By developing local energy error estimates together with energy estimates for a regularized Green's function, we establish a unified framework to prove the maximum-norm stability of both the semigroup defined by the semi-discrete scheme and the corresponding discrete solutions. The stability analysis shows that the main challenges stem from the treatment of numerical flux variables and the inherent asymmetry of the discrete scheme. These challenges are intrinsic to numerical approaches formulated within the mixed framework for parabolic equations. The asymmetry prevents the direct application of the double kick-back argument, while the presence of flux variables requires special techniques to control their values at the initial time. We emphasize that the analytical tools developed here to address these challenges are sufficiently general to be adapted for maximum-norm stability investigations of a broader class of numerical schemes arising from mixed formulations of parabolic equations. Furthermore, by the stability results and their proofs, we derive the maximal regularity of the semi-discrete solution in $L^{\infty}((0,T);L^p(Ω))$-norm and reduce the maximum-norm error estimates to those of the corresponding elliptic equations and the $L^2$-orthogonal projection. Since the derivation of the local energy error estimates does not rely on the lifting operator, which is only available for simplicial meshes, our results (excluding mixed methods) remain valid for polygonal/polyhedral meshes.

</details>


### [134] [The High Order Virtual Element Method On Approximate Domains: Neumann Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07100)
*Silvia Bertoluzza,Monica Montardini,Daniele Prada*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于处理虚拟元方法中曲边几何的Neumann边界条件的新方法，适用于通过底层精细结构网格聚集获得的域近似，并能证明任意阶离散的最优误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
